28mm Kodak 500T. cinematography. Very low golden-hour sun from the far side, hard backlight, long shadows, dust in the air, cool shade under the concrete deck. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no plastic skin. 24fps, 180-degree shutter. SFX only no music. No subtitles. 10s. Scene context: A concrete underpass in Los Angeles at the end of the day. A black coupe has just put itself sideways across the open asphalt. Now the doors open and four people climb out of it, awkwardly, because it only has two doors. As the last of them straightens up, a motorcycle comes in behind them and stops on their side. Three more people stand watching from the far side and never move. OPENING FRAME - begins EXACTLY here, matching the previous shot: @image1 sitting BROADSIDE across the LEFT and CENTRE of frame, engine off, dust still settling around it, doors shut. Over in the RIGHT third, three figures in burgundy stand watching, still. A wide empty gap of asphalt between them. @image1 - the car: 1964 BLACK TWO-DOOR hardtop coupe, chrome bumpers and side spear, wire wheels with whitewalls, parked broadside. @image2 - the driver: man late twenties, olive skin, black hair with a skin fade, dense black beard, glossy ORANGE wraparound sunglasses, BLACK track jacket with curved off-white panels. He carries a matte-black cinema camera with a pistol grip. @image3 - Black man, black paisley headband, full black beard, OLIVE bomber with orange BC, white tee, silver chain, baggy dark jeans. @image4 - very large heavyset Black man, black bowler hat, thick black-rimmed glasses, OLIVE short-sleeve shirt with orange BC, baggy jeans. @image5 - blonde woman, high long ponytail, narrow silver tinted sunglasses, OLIVE crop top with orange BC, denim shorts, a light wooden baseball bat in her hand. @image6 - East Asian man, low black ponytail, colour irezumi neck collar, OLIVE bomber, dark jeans, plain matte-black full-face helmet with the visor DOWN. @image7 - his motorcycle: glossy BLACK supersport with orange pinstripes and orange rim tape. @image8 - standing far right, watching: East Asian woman, high long black ponytail, colour tattoo sleeves, BURGUNDY crop top, beige cargo shorts, a sheathed katana in her hand, beside a dark burgundy motorcycle. Two more burgundy figures stand beside her - a broad blond man in a burgundy vest with a bandana, and a man in a long black beanie with a skateboard. @image9 - location: concrete overpass on massive square columns overhead, wide open asphalt with faded markings, sandy spoil banks, low industrial buildings, poles and palms far behind, low sun in bars between the columns. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY EIGHT PEOPLE BY THE END - FIVE on the LEFT (@image2 @image3 @image4 @image5 @image6) and THREE on the RIGHT. @image2 = ONLY orange sunglasses and black tracksuit. @image3 = ONLY black headband and beard. @image4 = ONLY bowler hat and heavy build. @image5 = ONLY blonde ponytail and bat. @image6 = ONLY the black helmet, and he is the ONLY person wearing one. No extras, no crowd, no duplicates. Nobody crosses to the other side. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. Wide, 28mm, chest height at ground level, holding the same framing as the previous shot: car broadside left and centre, empty gap through the middle, the burgundy three in the right third. Shoulder-mounted handheld with soft operator weight - slow live float, small roll, tiny corrections. It never pans away, never pushes in, never racks focus, never cuts to anyone. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-2.0s The driver's door swings open and @image2 gets out, straightens, and reaches back in to TIP HIS SEATBACK FORWARD, because there are no rear doors. He steps clear. 2.0-5.0s The three from the back bench climb out one at a time through that gap, unhurried and slightly awkward: @image3 first, dragging himself through; then @image5, ducking out with the bat in one hand; then @image4 last, who takes real effort to get his bulk through the opening,

ONE WOODEN KICKER RAMP stands on the asphalt to the right - plywood, waist height, scuffed sloping face - with @image6 PARKED BROADSIDE immediately beyond it, side-on to camera, its full length in the landing path. Both are there from the first frame and neither moves. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: ONLY THREE PEOPLE, ONE RAMP, ONE CAR. @image2 = ONLY long beanie and BURGUNDY jacket, always on the SKATEBOARD. @image4 = ONLY black headband and OLIVE bomber, always on the BMX. They never swap machines and are never airborne together. The ramp's shape, size and position NEVER change. Soft unresolved figures may sit at the frame edges. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS. 28mm, LOW, knee height, off to the side so the run reads left to right in profile. Shoulder-mounted handheld, step-bounce, small roll, never smooth. It holds the two men for seven seconds, then ONE FAST WHIP PAN right along the pointing finger, smearing, landing on the ramp AND the car behind it. After that it stays there, holding ramp and car in frame, panning to follow each rider left to right. It never whips again, cuts or pushes in. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-0.6s They stand facing each other. @image2 holds his board upright, not paying attention. 0.6-4.0s @image1: "From here to the kicker. Hit it clean, throw one trick, stick the landing." 4.0-5.6s @image2 looks straight ahead, then turns his WHOLE HEAD slowly left, holds, and turns it slowly all the way right. Genuinely searching, he finds nothing. 5.6-6.4s @image2, honestly puzzled: "Which kicker?" 6.4-7.4s @image1 does not answer. He raises one arm and points with one finger off right. 7.4-7.8s WHIP PAN along that finger, everything smearing. 7.8-9.0s It lands on the ramp with the black coupe parked broadside behind it. 9.0-9.6s From OFF FRAME, @image2, quietly: "Oh." 9.6-11.6s @image2 rides in fast from the left on @image3 and hits the ramp at an ANGLE, launching to the SIDE of the car, not over it. 11.6-12.4s In the air he KICKFLIPS: the board spins once fully beneath him and he catches it under his feet. 12.4-13.6s He LANDS clean beside the car and rolls out of frame right. 13.6-14.8s Ramp and car sit alone. 14.8-16.6s @image4 enters from left already rolling on @image5 and cranks HARD, three fast strokes, standing on the pedals, driving STRAIGHT at it. 16.6-17.6s He hits it at full speed, plywood knocking, and LAUNCHES off the lip. 17.6-18.8s FLIGHT: he arcs OVER the parked coupe, bike tucked under him, passing clearly above the roofline, the whole car sliding beneath him. 18.8-20.0s He LANDS beyond it, rear wheel then front, knees absorbing hard, rolling straight. 20.0-21.4s He lets the speed die and looks back over his shoulder at the car he cleared. 21.4-23.0s He rides a short arc and stops, foot down. BOTH RIDERS ARE GENUINELY GOOD, never comic, neither celebrates. The kickflip is exactly one full rotation. The BMX leaves the lip on a ballistic curve peaking over the middle of the car; the flight is brief and fast, exactly as long as physics allows - never suspended, never floating. Suspension compresses on the ramp and hard on landing. Wheels never strobe. @image2's eyes are hidden behind dark sunglasses, so his search plays in slow full head turns with pauses at each end, never darting eyes, never a comic double-take. He genuinely cannot see it. @image1 shows nothing. Nobody smiles. Identity and skin stay constant. ONLY @image1 AND @image2 SPEAK, AND ONLY THE LINES ABOVE. @image4 never speaks. No ad-libs, mumbling, grunts, whoops or laughs; breathing is body sound only. Every beat lands on the tail of the one before. SFX - wind, grit, a rush of air on the whip pan, the board clacking onto asphalt, hard pushes, wheels roaring, the hollow knock of the ramp, a hard slap as wheels land, then BMX cranks and chain under load, the ramp knocking again, clean air, a double slap on landing, freewheel ticking down, breathing. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: nothing transforms or materialises - ramp and car are there from the first frame and never change shape, size or position. Never more than one ramp. The whip pan

## FULL PROMPT

14S · ONE TAKE · 5 REFERENCES

Kodak 500T. cinematography. Very low golden-hour sun, warm light, long shadows, dust in the air. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no plastic skin. 24fps, 180-degree shutter. SFX only no music. No subtitles. 14s. @image1 - ALWAYS frame LEFT: blonde woman, dark roots, high long ponytail, NO SUNGLASSES, eyes fully visible, OLIVE crop top with orange BC, denim shorts, white sneakers. Breathing hard. @image2 - HER weapon: light wooden baseball bat, pale ash, thin burnt-orange band,

BOOTCAMP VS SLOP DAWGZ

104

<!-- page 105 -->

scuffed with fresh surface marks. @image3 - ALWAYS frame RIGHT: East Asian woman, high long black ponytail, nose ring, full colour irezumi sleeves and tattooed neck with a lotus at the throat, BURGUNDY crop top, beige cargo shorts, black high-tops, burgundy bandana round her right forearm. Breathing hard. @image4 - HER weapon: katana with a bare steel blade, dark burgundy cord-wrapped handle, dark openwork guard, already drawn. @image5 - location: open cracked asphalt before a concrete overpass on square columns, sandy spoil banks, low sun. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY TWO PEOPLE IN FRAME. @image1 in OLIVE stays on the LEFT for the whole shot, @image3 in BURGUNDY stays on the RIGHT. They never swap sides. The bat is only ever with @image1, the blade only ever with @image3. Soft unresolved figures may sit at the frame edges. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. 35mm, chest height, on the SAME SIDE of the line between them as before. For the first eight seconds it TRACKS LATERALLY with the fight, full-body, hands and weapons always visible, occasionally a fraction late to a fast movement. At 8.0s, the moment they both stop, the camera SETTLES and eases in slightly to a static medium two-shot where both faces and both sets of eyes are readable, and holds there completely still for the rest of the shot. It never crosses the line, never circles, never cuts, never racks focus. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-2.6s The exchange is already running fast. @image1 drives forward with two more heavy swings, using pure physical pressure, and @image3 gives ground across the asphalt - balanced, unhurried, never touched. 2.6-4.8s @image3 steps in with two quick precise movements that force @image1 to defend, and the pressure reverses for a moment. 4.8-7.0s @image1 takes it back with a hard low swing. @image3 turns it aside and comes straight back at the body. 7.0-8.0s BOTH LAND AT ONCE. @image1's bat stops DEAD in the air a few centimetres from @image3's head, held there by her own arms. At exactly the same instant @image3's blade is already lying flat against @image1's ribs, edge turned away, arrived first and stopped. 8.0-10.0s Neither of them moves at all. The camera settles. Both are breathing hard, weapons still in position, both fully aware that the next movement means both of them get hit. 10.0-11.4s They look at each other. Nothing else. No taunt, no smile, no words. 11.4-12.4s @image3 gives one short nod. @image1 returns it, just as short. It is respect and nothing more. 12.4-14.0s BOTH LOWER THEIR WEAPONS AT THE SAME TIME, unhurried, in one movement. The bat comes down, the blade drops away from her ribs. They stand there, still facing each other, and neither of them has won. PHYSICS: both weapons have real weight and both stops are real - the bat is halted by muscle and momentum, not by magic, and it visibly shudders as it is arrested. The blade lies flat against the body with the edge deliberately turned away. Feet scuff and slide on grit, bodies stay balanced and grounded. Nothing touches skin hard enough to mark it. ACTING: no comedy anywhere and no triumph on either side. Both are properly winded, chests rising and falling, hair stuck to damp foreheads. @image1's eyes are visible, hard, then gradually settling into recognition. @image3's eyes are calm and level, reading the situation before anyone else does. The nod is small, brief and unsentimental - neither of them smiles. Identity and skin stay constant. NOBODY SPEAKS. No dialogue, war cries, shouts, screams, grunts-as-voice, ad-libs or laughs. No lips move to words at any point. SFX - NO MUSIC OF ANY KIND. Only: feet driving and sliding on grit, air moving as the bat swings, two hard cracks of wood against steel during the exchange, then abrupt QUIET as they both stop, both women breathing hard and close, fabric shifting, a faint metallic ring dying in the blade, wind through the underpass. The silence after the stop is the point. STRICT NEGATIVES: no music, score, drone or tension bed at any point. Neither of them is ever actually hit, cut, wounded, knocked down or disarmed. Nobody bleeds. The bat never touches her head and the blade never cuts skin. The blade never splinters or severs the bat. No weapon is dropped, thrown, spun or twirled. No sparks. No acrobatics, flips, spinning kicks, jumping attacks

BOOTCAMP VS SLOP DAWGZ 105

<!-- page 106 -->

or wire-work. Neither woman wins, celebrates, gloats, apologises, bows deeply or shakes hands - the nod is the whole exchange. Nobody smiles. No cut, insert, close-up or reverse angle. The camera never crosses the line between them, never circles, orbits, rises or racks focus, and once it settles at 8.0s it does not move again. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze - everything plays at full speed. No relighting.

ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-1.6s Both are watching something OFF FRAME to the left, out where the fight just ended. @image1's jaw is set hard. Identity and skin texture stay constant. Both breathe; nobody freezes. ONLY @image1 AND @image3 SPEAK, AND ONLY THE LINES ABOVE. No ad-libs, mumbling, grunts, breaths-as-voice, chuckles or laughs. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. 35mm, LOW, close to the ground, angled slightly up so that the asphalt, the men, the overpass deck and a large area of OPEN SKY above it are all inside the same frame. ACTION, TIMED - THE TRANSFORMATION IS NEVER CUT AND NEVER HIDDEN: 0.0-2.0s @image1 crouches over the concrete block lying on the asphalt, gets both arms under it and STRAIGHTENS UP with it against his chest. It is genuinely heavy - his back sets, his feet plant wide, the effort is real. 2.0-2.8s @image2 gives a short flick of the chin. Nothing more. 2.8-4.0s @image1 turns his hips and HURLS the block straight up. It leaves his arms fast, tumbling slowly end over end, and climbs high into the open sky above the overpass, dust coming off it. 4.0-5.0s @image2 raises @image3 in both hands, tracks the block with the barrel, and waits. 5.0-5.6s He fires. THE BLAST: a short dense pulse of energy with a hot amber-white core wrapped in an almost transparent burgundy distortion of space. The air along its path physically refracts and bends. Dust and grit lift off the ground in the pressure wave. A brief volumetric scattering of light, a small optical bloom. It is dense and physical, never a cheap neon laser beam. 5.6-6.0s The pulse strikes the block at the top of its arc, high in the air. 6.0-7.6s TRANSFORMATION IN FREE FALL, ONE UNBROKEN MOVEMENT: a generative membrane sweeps through the tumbling block and it rebuilds itself as it falls - grey concrete breaking into fine particles, then hide, then dense fur, then legs, then a tail, then a head with a heavy mane. It never disappears, is never obscured, and stays visible in frame every single frame. 7.6-8.4s @image4 lands HARD on the asphalt on all four paws, the full weight of a real big cat driving into the ground, dust exploding outward from the impact, the whole body absorbing the landing through its shoulders. 8.4-10.0s It rises to its full height and stands there, enormous and completely real, breathing, mane shifting in the wind. Nobody moves. Nobody says anything. LIGHT AND MATERIAL: the only light source is the low golden sun. Wind through the underpass, distant freeway traffic. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: NO CUT AT ANY POINT, above all not during the transformation. The dividing line is hard and clean, not blended. @image2 - frame RIGHT, standing back: broad white man, spiked bleached blond hair, BURGUNDY paisley bandana, narrow black sunglasses, BURGUNDY sleeveless quilted vest, grey tattoo sleeve. @image3 - HIS device, hanging low in one hand: battered pistol-shaped blaster, burgundy paint chipped to bare steel, warm amber tubes inside. @image4 - frame LEFT, standing back: large heavyset Black man, full round afro, thick full black beard, BURGUNDY paisley bandana round his forehead, black denim trucker jacket over a long white tee. @image5 - THE LOCATION AND THE EXACT DIRECTION THE CAMERA IS FACING. 116

<!-- page 117 -->

on either side and open asphalt around it. Shoulder-mounted handheld, gentle float, small roll, tiny corrections. IT NEVER CUTS AWAY AND NEVER SHORTENS THE MOMENT - the audience is made to watch the whole failure without relief. It never pushes in, racks focus, pans away or goes to a close-up. ACTION, TIMED - THE FRONT AND BACK OF THE ANIMAL DO NOT BELONG TO THE SAME MOVEMENT: 0.0-1.4s The lion stands still, half fur and half plastic. It shifts its weight as if about to walk. 1.4-3.2s IT TRIES TO WALK. The front legs take two proper strides forward. The BACK LEGS DO NOT COME WITH THEM - they stay planted where they were, as if they belong to a different animal that has not been told. The torso stretches out absurdly long between them, the spine extending far beyond anything anatomical. 3.2-4.2s Then the back half SNAPS forward all at once, catching up in a single unnatural jerk, and the body compresses back to its correct length. 4.2-5.4s The lion is now standing in almost exactly the pose it started in, having gone nowhere at all. It shifts its weight again, ready to try once more. A LOOP. 5.4-7.2s IT TRIES TO JUMP. The front of the body launches correctly - chest lifting, forelegs leaving the ground, head coming up with real power. The back half performs a completely different action at the same time, as if running a separate movement, legs going sideways instead of driving upward. 7.2-8.6s The two halves fight each other. It trips over its own body, the plastic side scraping the asphalt, and lands awkwardly and heavily on its side of the chest. 8.6-10.2s It stops dead. Completely motionless except for its breathing, standing in a wrong, unbalanced pose it clearly cannot resolve. 10.2-12.0s Nothing happens. It just stands there, broken, in the low sun. @image2 does not move. Nobody speaks. THE FAILURE IS PHYSICAL, NOT MAGICAL: the animal has real weight and the ground is real. Dust lifts where the paws land and where the plastic side scrapes. The stretching, the snap-back and the split jump are errors of animation, not of physics - the body behaves like something being driven by two different systems that are not talking to each other. ACTING: @image2's eyes are hidden behind dark lenses; his confidence drains out through the jaw alone, slowly, over the whole shot. He never speaks, never laughs, never looks at @image4. @image4's eyes are visible and he simply watches without expression. Neither of them intervenes, approaches or reacts loudly. NOBODY SPEAKS. No dialogue, ad-libs, mumbling, grunts-as-voice, laughs or offscreen voices. No lips move to words at any point. SFX - heavy paws landing unevenly on asphalt, a dry plastic scrape as the moulded side drags across the ground, the animal's breathing, grit and dust, wind moving low, distant freeway traffic. NO roar, NO growl, NO big-cat sound of any kind. No music, no comic sting, no cartoon sound effect. STRICT NEGATIVES: NO CUT AT ANY POINT - the failure plays out in one unbroken shot and the camera never looks away, never shortens it, never covers it with a foreground object. The lion never walks normally, never moves correctly, never recovers. It never roars, growls, attacks, charges or threatens anyone. It never leaves frame. The fur does not dissolve, fragment or glitch into digital pieces in this shot - the body stays solid, half fur and half plastic, throughout. Nobody laughs, points, runs, screams or steps toward it. No cut, insert, close-up, reverse angle or second camera position. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze - everything plays at full speed. No gimbal, dolly, crane or drone. No relighting.

## FULL PROMPT

10S · ONE TAKE · 5 REFERENCES

Kodak 500T. cinematography. Very low golden-hour sun, warm light, long shadows, dust. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no CGI sheen. 24fps, 180-degree shutter. SFX only no music. No subtitles. 10s. OPENING FRAME - begins EXACTLY here: the lion LYING ON ITS SIDE on the cracked asphalt, motionless, almost entirely yellow plastic now with only the muzzle and one front paw still real fur. @image2 stands over it at frame right looking down, @image4 at frame left looking down. Neither is moving. @image1 - the animal on the ground: an enormous male LION collapsed on its side, most of the body now smooth matte YELLOW PLASTIC with moulded seams and simplified forms, one glassy round eye open, only the muzzle and one forepaw still carrying real photographic fur. @image2 - frame RIGHT, standing over it, looking down: broad white man, spiked bleached blond hair, BURGUNDY paisley bandana, narrow black sunglasses, BURGUNDY sleeveless quilted vest, grey tattoo sleeve. @image3 - HIS device, hanging low in one hand: battered pistol-shaped blaster, burgundy paint chipped to bare steel, warm amber tubes inside. @image4 - frame LEFT, standing back, looking down: large heavyset Black man, full round afro, thick full black beard, BURGUNDY paisley bandana round his forehead, black denim trucker jacket over a long white tee. @image5 - THE LOCATION AND THE EXACT DIRECTION THE CAMERA IS FACING. Use it for the ground, the structure, the horizon, the light and the colour. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY TWO PEOPLE AND, UNTIL 8.6s, ONE ANIMAL. AFTER THAT THERE IS NO ANIMAL AT ALL. Nobody else in frame. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. 35mm, chest height, a wide holding the whole fallen animal inside frame with both men standing over it. Shoulder-mounted handheld, gentle float, small roll, tiny corrections. It never pushes in, never racks focus, never cuts, never looks away from the body. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-1.6s The lion lies there, not moving. Only the low sun and the dust. Neither man says anything or touches it. 1.6-3.4s The surface begins to FAIL. Along the plastic flank the material starts breaking into small hard-edged fragments that separate slightly from each other, like a shell coming apart. The remaining patch of real fur on the muzzle flickers between sharp and soft. 3.4-5.0s The OUTLINE stops holding. The silhouette wavers and re-forms, the edges of the legs blurring and snapping back, parts of the shape briefly not matching the rest of it. 5.0-6.4s It gets worse fast. Fragments detach and hang for an instant. The head loses its shape. The colour flattens and drains out of the plastic. 6.4-7.4s THE WHOLE ANIMAL COMES APART INTO PARTICLES. Starting at the head and running down the length

BOOTCAMP VS SLOP DAWGZ

120

<!-- page 121 -->

of the body, the surface breaks into a dense cloud of fine dry fragments - flat matte flecks of yellow plastic and dulled fur, no bigger than grains of sand - and the shape unravels from front to back. The particles do not rise: they blow outward and DOWNWARD, carried sideways by the wind, losing what colour they had as they go. 7.4-8.6s The last of the body dissolves into the cloud and there is nothing left but a low drifting haze where it lay. The particles fall through the low sun, catching it dully, and begin settling onto the cracked asphalt. 8.6-10.0s The haze thins out and blows away across the open ground. @image2 and @image4 are still looking at the empty ground. Neither of them moves. Complete silence. THE PARTICLES ARE DEAD MATTER, NOT MAGIC: they are dry, matte and colourless, the debris of something that failed rather than something being released. They never glow, never sparkle, never emit light, never rise upward and never swirl gracefully. They fall, drift sideways with the wind and settle on the ground like ash. ACTING: @image2's eyes are hidden behind dark lenses; he simply stands there looking down at nothing. @image4's eyes are visible and he shows almost nothing. Neither of them speaks, laughs, reacts, steps back or touches anything. Identity and skin texture stay constant. NOBODY SPEAKS. No dialogue, ad-libs, mumbling, grunts, breaths-as-voice, laughs or offscreen voices. No lips move to words at any point. SFX - wind moving low across open ground, a dry brittle crackling as the surface breaks apart, then a long soft granular hiss as the whole body comes apart into particles and blows sideways, particles settling onto asphalt like sand. No whoosh, no pop, no chime, no electronic sound. Distant freeway traffic. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: the particles never glow, sparkle, emit light, catch fire or turn golden. Nothing rises upward, nothing floats away into the sky, nothing evaporates or turns to light. No embers, fireflies, magic dust, digital cubes, wireframes, hexagons, scan lines or hologram effects. The animal never re-forms, never gets up, never comes back, and no second animal appears. Nobody laughs, points, screams, runs or touches the ground. No cut, insert, close-up or reverse angle - the camera holds the same frame throughout. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze. No gimbal, dolly, crane or drone. No relighting.

12S · ONE TAKE · 5 REFERENCES

Kodak 500T. Very low golden-hour sun, warm light, long shadows, dust. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no CGI sheen. 24fps, 180-degree shutter. SFX only no music. No subtitles. American accent. 12s. @image1 - THE RING, filling the frame at the start: a massive gold signet ring, densely pave-set with small stones across its whole face, worn on a thick finger, the low sun breaking into hundreds of small warm reflections across the stones. @image2 - the man wearing it: very large heavyset Black man, black bowler hat, thick black-rimmed glasses with clear lenses, short greying beard, OLIVE short-sleeve shirt with orange BC. His hand and forearm fill the opening frame; his face is only seen later. @image3 - further away in the depth: East Asian man, black hair in a low ponytail, colour irezumi neck collar, OLIVE bomber with orange BC, standing beside his motorcycle. @image4 - his motorcycle: glossy black supersport, orange pinstripes and orange rim tape, on its side stand. @image5 - THE LOCATION AND THE EXACT DIRECTION THE CAMERA IS FACING. Use it for the ground, the structure, the horizon, the light and the colour. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY TWO PEOPLE. @image2 in the near ground, @image3 further away in the depth. Nobody else in frame. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. THE CONCRETE: several heavy grey CONCRETE BLOCKS lying on the cracked asphalt, rough cast surfaces, chipped corners, rusted reinforcing bar stubs, dirt and dust on them. Ordinary construction debris. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. It begins as a MACRO EXTREME CLOSE-UP on the ring, 85mm, held very close, the stones filling frame with everything beyond them soft. Then, at 3.4s, it TILTS DOWN AND ACROSS in one smooth motivated movement - following where the change is happening - and settles into a wide holding the concrete blocks, the open asphalt and @image3 in the depth. That is the only camera move. It never cuts, never whips, never pushes in, never racks focus back to the ring. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-1.8s MACRO on the ring. The low sun moves across the pave stones as the hand shifts slightly, breaking into dozens of small warm reflections. Nothing else happens. 1.8-3.0s His THUMB turns the ring on his finger - barely, a few degrees, an ordinary unconscious movement. For a fraction of a second, deep inside the reflections in the stones, a fine geometric structure appears and is gone. No glow, no neon, no light emitted. 3.0-3.4s Around the ring the air itself DEFORMS very slightly, an almost invisible transparent distortion, like heat over asphalt but colder and more ordered. 3.4-5.0s The camera TILTS DOWN AND ACROSS, following that distortion as it spreads outward low over the ground toward the concrete blocks, arriving on them in a wide. 5.0-6.4s The distortion reaches the blocks and passes through them as a thin generative membrane, almost completely silent. Where it passes, the concrete breaks into fine dry particles. 6.4-9.0s THE MATERIAL CHANGES IN ONE

UNBROKEN MOVEMENT. The particles resettle as timber: heavy planks, a braced frame, a long angled ramp face. What forms is not a small kicker but a LARGE PROFESSIONAL WOODEN STUNT RAMP - tall, wide, properly built, with a long sloping run-up surface and a solid supporting structure, big enough to launch a full-size sportbike. 9.0-10.4s The last particles land. The ramp stands there, solid and physically convincing, dust drifting off it. Nothing glows. 10.4-11.2s In the depth, @image2's face turns toward @image3. Flat, quiet: "You got it." 11.2-12.0s @image3 is already moving toward @image4. Nobody else says anything. THE OPPOSITE OF A DISPLAY: nothing about this is theatrical. He never raises his hand, never aims, never points, never gestures at anything, never looks at the ring and never watches his own work. The whole thing happens low, quietly, and almost off-handedly. No beam, no blast, no flash, no recoil, no sound of a weapon. LIGHT AND MATERIAL: the only light source is the low golden sun. Nothing emits light at any point. The membrane is a transparent deformation of air, not a glowing sheet. The particles are dry matte flecks of concrete and then wood, catching the sunset dully. The finished ramp is real timber - visible grain, saw marks, bolt heads, dust - with a correct hard shadow on the asphalt. ONLY @image2 SPEAKS, ONE SHORT LINE, AT THE END. @image3 never speaks. No ad-libs, mumbling, grunts, breaths-as-voice, chuckles or laughs. Every beat lands on the tail of the one before; nothing is held longer than written. SFX - wind moving low across open ground, the tiny dry click of a ring turning on a finger, then an almost inaudible low pressure shift as the air deforms, a soft granular hiss as concrete becomes particles and particles become timber, wood settling and creaking once as it takes its own weight, dust. Distant freeway traffic. No music, no electronic tone, no whoosh, no impact bang. STRICT NEGATIVES: NO CUT AT ANY POINT, especially not during the change. Nothing passes in front to hide it - no whip pan, flash frame, white-out or smoke cloud. The blocks are visible throughout the transformation and never disappear and reappear. Nothing glows, sparkles, flashes, emits a beam or throws light onto anyone. No neon, no lightning, no fireball, no shockwave. @image2 never raises his hand, points, aims or performs any gesture. The ramp never appears instantly out of nothing - it is built from the material of the blocks. It is a LARGE stunt ramp, never a small kicker. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze. No gimbal, dolly, crane or drone. No relighting.

THE RAMP MUST MATCH IT EXACTLY: heavy pale timber, braced frame of uprights and diagonal struts, visible bolt heads, long curved run-up surface, same size and same proportions. Its construction never changes. @image2 - the rider: East Asian man, black hair in a low ponytail, colour irezumi neck collar, OLIVE bomber with orange BC, dark jeans. He pulls on a plain matte-black full-face helmet at the start and the dark visor stays DOWN from then on; his face is not visible after that. @image3 - his motorcycle: glossy BLACK supersport with orange pinstripes and orange rim tape, titanium exhaust. IT IS COMPLETELY CONSISTENT FOR THE WHOLE SHOT - identical shape, proportions and colour in every frame, nothing about it ever morphs, stretches or re-forms. @image4 - standing still and watching at the SIDE of frame, in the middle distance: East Asian woman, high long black ponytail, colour irezumi sleeves, BURGUNDY crop top, beige cargo shorts, a sheathed katana at her side. @image5 - location reference for the surroundings, the horizon, the light and the colour. Soft unresolved figures may stand at the extreme frame edges. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. THE DIRECTION OF THE RUN: the ramp faces LEFT, so the approach comes across the open ground toward it and the launch carries out over the empty asphalt beyond. The whole flight path stays inside the frame. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. The shot OPENS ALREADY TIGHT AND CLOSE - a low 35mm framing on the rider and the machine as he pulls the helmet on, the ramp large and looming in the background behind him. It does NOT start wide. From there it TRACKS LATERALLY with him as he accelerates, widening naturally as he covers ground, holding steady through the launch, the flight and the landing. At the very end, after he has landed and is riding away, it RISES SMOOTHLY AND SLOWLY - a light crane-up - opening the scale of the whole space. That rise is the only vertical move. It never cuts, whips, pushes in or racks focus. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-1.4s CLOSE on @image2 as he pulls the helmet down and drops the visor, the machine beneath him, the ramp standing large behind him out

of focus. ## FULL PROMPT

10S · ONE TAKE · 6 REFERENCES

Kodak 500T. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no CGI sheen. 24fps, 180-degree shutter. SFX only no music. No subtitles. 10s. Scene context: An open asphalt lot in Los Angeles at the end of the day, near the columns of a concrete overpass. A man fires a device into a pile of scrap and it becomes a black sportbike. It looks perfect. Then something about it is wrong. A woman stands a very long way off at the other end of the lot, nowhere near any of it. @image1 - NEAR GROUND at frame LEFT, sharp: broad white man, spiked bleached blond hair, BURGUNDY paisley bandana, narrow black sunglasses, heavy jaw, thin scar under his right eye, BURGUNDY sleeveless quilted vest over a white tee, grey tattoo sleeve, beige cargo trousers. @image2 - HIS device, held only by @image1: battered pistol-shaped blaster, burgundy paint chipped to bare steel, perforated barrel shroud, small scope, warm amber glass tubes glowing inside. @image3 - VERY FAR AWAY at frame RIGHT, small but readable, standing at the far end of the lot: East Asian woman, high long black ponytail, colour irezumi sleeves and tattooed neck, BURGUNDY crop top, beige cargo shorts, a sheathed katana at her side. She stands beside her own motorcycle and never moves from that spot. @image4 - HER motorcycle, parked beside her at the far end: glossy DARK BURGUNDY supersport, black hardware, on its side stand. @image5 - THE ENVIRONMENT AND THE DIRECTION THE CAMERA IS FACING. Use it for the ground, the concrete overpass and its square columns, the parked maroon van, the horizon, the light and the colour. @image6 - additional environment and spatial continuity reference. It is NOT the first frame of this shot. Use it for the light, the colour grade and how the space reads. All character cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. THE SCRAP PILE, present until 4.0s: a heap of old junk lying against the base of a concrete column - a rusted supermarket trolley on its side, a crumpled sheet-metal cowling, lengths of bent reinforcing bar, dust and weeds growing through it. THE SPORTBIKE, from 4.0s onward: a modern black supersport motorcycle, deep gloss black paint with visible clearcoat depth, clean weld seams on the frame, drilled brake discs, black wheels, the low sun sitting in a long highlight across the fuel tank. Beautifully rendered, expensive-looking and completely convincing to look at. It has NO side stand and nothing is holding it up. STRICT: EXACTLY TWO PEOPLE. @image1 large in the near ground at frame LEFT, @image3 very small at frame RIGHT at the far end of the lot with @image4 beside her. THE DISTANCE BETWEEN THEM IS THE POINT - she is at least twenty metres away from the scrap and stays there for the entire shot. Soft unresolved figures may stand at the extreme frame edges, alive and idle, never in focus,

never identified. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. A short dense pulse with a hot amber-white core wrapped in an almost transparent burgundy distortion of air. The air along its path refracts and bends. Dust lifts off the ground in the pressure wave. 2.8-4.0s A generative membrane sweeps through the scrap. The rusted metal breaks into fine dry particles and resettles, and where the heap was there is now a BLACK SPORTBIKE, complete and immaculate, the low sun running along its tank. 4.0-5.6s It looks flawless. Paint, welds, discs, everything. The answer to what came before reads instantly. 5.6-7.0s Then the wrongness. THE MOTORCYCLE IS STANDING PERFECTLY UPRIGHT ON BOTH WHEELS. There is no side stand, no support and nobody touching it. It is not leaning at all. It simply stands there balanced, which no motorcycle can do. 7.0-9.0s THE FRONT WHEEL BEGINS TO TURN. SFX - boots on grit, a short dense concussive discharge with a low pressure thump rather than a laser whine, a dry granular rush as the scrap becomes particles and the bike settles, then near silence, and under it a faint continuous mechanical whirr as the front wheel turns with nothing driving it. Wind moving low, distant freeway traffic. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: the blast is never a thin neon laser line, never a glowing beam, never lightning, never a fireball or explosion. Nothing catches fire. The bike NEVER falls over, tips, moves position or is touched in this shot. It never has a side stand and nobody holds it. The rear wheel NEVER turns at any point - only the front one does. The bike never starts, never revs, never makes an engine sound. @image3 never walks toward the scrap, never crosses the lot, never speaks and never leaves her spot at the far end. The camera never pushes in on the wheel, never cuts to a close-up of it, never pans away from the wide. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze. No gimbal, dolly, crane or drone. No relighting.

ACTION, TIMED - THREE SEPARATE FAILURES, EACH FULLY READABLE: 0.0-0.8s The motorcycle stands there upright with nothing holding it. @image2 glances toward the people off frame and moves quickly - he wants this over before anyone looks closely. 0.8-1.4s He takes hold of the handlebars with both hands and PUSHES the bike forward. 1.4-3.2s FIRST FAILURE: it moves. But the WHEELS DO NOT ROTATE AT ALL, not by a single degree. The entire machine SLIDES across the asphalt as one rigid object, like a piece of furniture being shoved, dragging with a dry grinding scrape. The tyres do not deform, do not roll, do not turn. 3.2-4.0s He stops pushing, lets go of the bars and steps back half a pace. 4.0-5.8s SECOND FAILURE: the HANDLEBARS TURN. Slowly, on their own, a good twenty degrees to one side. THE FRAME DOES NOT FOLLOW THEM. The forks, the front wheel and the whole front end stay exactly where they are while the bars rotate independently, disconnected from the machine they belong to. 5.8-7.2s THIRD FAILURE, NOT CUT AND NOT HIDDEN: the bike TIPS AND FALLS SIDEWAYS. As it falls it does not behave like mass - it holds its exact shape rigidly the whole way down, with no compression, no bounce, no suspension travel, no weight shift, as though a flat image is toppling rather than a physical object. 7.2-8.8s It hits the asphalt and lies there. THE OUTLINE STOPS HOLDING: the edges waver and re-form, the shape breaks into hard-edged fragments, the colour drains, and the whole thing comes apart into dry matte particles that blow outward and downward and settle. 8.8-10.0s Lying in its place on the asphalt is the RUSTED SUPERMARKET TROLLEY, on its side, exactly where the machine was. 10.0-11.0s One caster wheel on the trolley keeps turning slowly, ticking. Wind moving low, distant freeway traffic. No music, no glitch sound, no electronic tone. STRICT NEGATIVES: NO CUT AT ANY POINT, especially not during the fall and the collapse. The bike never bounces, never compresses, never behaves like it has weight, and the suspension never travels. Nothing catches fire, no fuel spills, no sparks. The particles never glow, sparkle or rise upward - they fall and blow sideways. Scene context: An open asphalt lot in Los Angeles at the end of the day. A rider takes a large wooden ramp. While he is already in the air and can no longer stop, a man in the foreground raises a weapon and fires at his front wheel. A woman standing beside that man steps across the line of fire and cuts the shot out of the air. Everything happens in one unbroken shot. @image1 - ENVIRONMENT AND SPATIAL CONTINUITY REFERENCE. BLACK wheels and BLACK rims. It stays mechanically identical in every frame - same shape, same colour, nothing morphs or changes. @image4 - FOREGROUND, frame LEFT, closer to camera: broad white man, spiked bleached blond hair, BURGUNDY paisley bandana, narrow black sunglasses, BURGUNDY sleeveless quilted vest over a white tee, grey tattoo sleeve, beige cargo trousers. @image5 - HIS weapon: battered pistol-shaped blaster, burgundy paint chipped to bare steel, perforated barrel shroud, small scope, warm amber glass tubes inside, open metal muzzle. @image6 - FOREGROUND, standing close beside him: East Asian woman, high long black ponytail, nose ring, full colour irezumi sleeves and a colour tattooed neck, BURGUNDY ribbed crop top, beige cargo shorts, a BURGUNDY paisley bandana round her right forearm. @image7 - HER sword, worn at her LEFT hip and drawn during this shot: katana with a bare steel blade, dark burgundy cord-wrapped handle, dark openwork guard, dark burgundy lacquered scabbard. The blade is plain polished steel and stays plain polished steel throughout. @image8 - location reference for the ground, the overpass, the horizon, the low warm sun and the colour. All character cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or grey backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY THREE PEOPLE IN FRAME AND NOBODY ELSE. @image4 and @image6 in the foreground, @image2 on the motorcycle in the middle and far ground. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. THE THREE PLANES, ALL INSIDE ONE FRAME THE WHOLE TIME: the two people in the FOREGROUND at frame left; the wooden ramp in the MIDGROUND; the run-up, the flight path and the clear landing ground in the BACKGROUND. The audience sees the whole causal chain at once. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, ABSOLUTELY NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. 32mm, chest height, a wide three-plane framing. It follows the rider with a slow motivated lateral movement while keeping the two foreground figures inside frame at all times. It never cuts, whips, pushes in, racks focus or goes to a close-up. It carries the last of its instability up to the moment of the cut; FROM THE INSTANT SHE CUTS THE MEMBRANE the movement becomes perfectly

controlled and stays that way to the end. ACTION, TIMED - NOT ONE CUT FROM START TO FINISH: 0.0-3.0s @image2 accelerates hard along the run-up on @image3, engine climbing. In the foreground @image4 turns his body toward the ramp but keeps the weapon low along his thigh, hiding the movement. @image6 does not move. 3.0-4.0s @image2 hits the timber, runs up the ramp face and LAUNCHES. Front wheel leaves the lip, then the rear wheel clears the edge. 4.0-5.2s Only now, with the motorcycle FULLY IN THE AIR and the rider unable to brake or change anything, @image4 SNAPS the weapon up, aims at its front wheel and fires. 5.2-5.8s THE SHOT: a short dense generative pulse leaves the muzzle - a hot amber-white core inside an almost transparent BURGUNDY SPATIAL MEMBRANE. The air along its path physically refracts and bends. A short pressure wave lifts dust near him. Dense and physical, never a laser. 5.8-6.6s The pulse travels toward the airborne motorcycle. @image2 is in the air, looking ahead, and does not see it. 6.6-7.2s @image6 MOVES. One fast step sideways ACROSS THE LINE OF FIRE - her first physical step out of her own crew's space. In the same movement she draws @image7. 7.2-7.6s ONE SHORT DIAGONAL CUT. No wind-up, no overhead swing, no pose. The plain steel blade meets the flying membrane. The blade does not glow and does not change - it is still an ordinary metal sword. She cuts the thin spatial structure itself. 7.6-8.2s For a fraction of a second ALL SOUND DROPS INTO A VACUUM. The membrane SPLITS IN TWO. One half passes above the rider, the other below the motorcycle. Both change direction, strike bare concrete either side of the landing ground and collapse into short silent spatial deformations - a brief warp of space, a small wave of dust, a few fragments vanishing instantly. 8.2-9.6s Sound returns hard. @image2 LANDS CLEAN - front wheel first, then rear, suspension compressing fully and rebounding, tyres gripping. The machine keeps its exact shape and mass. 9.6-12.0s He rides on a few metres, stabilises, and only then looks back. @image6 stands between him and @image4 with the blade lowered. A last thin burgundy ripple runs down the steel and disappears. PHYSICS AND SCALE: no huge explosion, no fireball, no neon sparks, no shockwave ring. Only real spatial refraction, a short wave of dust and fragments that vanish. The motorcycle has real mass and follows a real ballistic arc that bends under gravity. The flight is brief and fast, exactly as long as physics allows. ACTING: @image4 hides the movement until the last possible second; his eyes are behind dark lenses so it plays in the shoulders and the hand. @image6 is completely economical - no heroic pose, no shout, no visible effort, just a step and a cut, as if she decided long before. @image2 never sees the shot coming and never reacts to it in the air. NOBODY SPEAKS. No dialogue, ad-libs, shouts, war cries, grunts-as-voice or laughs. No lips move to words at any point. SFX - engine winding up hard, timber drumming under the wheels, a moment of engine note with no tyre noise while airborne, then a short dense concussive discharge with a low pressure thump rather than a laser whine, the pulse travelling, then TOTAL SILENCE for a fraction of a second at the cut, then a sharp ring of steel and sound rushing back, a heavy landing thump with suspension bottoming and tyres chirping, then the engine pulling away. Wind, grit, distant freeway traffic. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: NO CUT AT ANY POINT, from launch to landing. Nothing passes in front to hide any part of it. The rider is never hit, never crashes, never falls, never wobbles and lands cleanly. The motorcycle never changes shape, colour or proportions and NEVER has orange wheels or orange rims. The shot is never a laser, never a straight beam of light, never lightning, never a fireball. The blade NEVER glows, lights up, becomes magical or changes appearance. No slow motion, speed ramp, freeze frame or bullet time anywhere - everything plays at full speed. She never poses, never raises the sword overhead, never shouts, never spins. Nobody else enters frame. No explosion, sparks, fire or shockwave ring. The camera never cuts, whips, pushes in, racks focus or becomes shakier after the cut. No gimbal, dolly, crane or drone. No relighting.

Nobody cheers, claps, jeers or celebrates. Natural skin with visible pores and faint shine in the low sun, no retouching. Identity and skin texture stay constant. The lettering never changes spelling, font, colour or position. @image2 - the car: 1964 BLACK hardtop coupe, long flat hood, chrome bumpers and side spear, quad round tail lights, wire wheels with whitewall tyres, sitting LOW. It OPENS VERY LOW, almost at road level, 28mm, close to the tarmac with the near lane filling the bottom of the frame and only the lower trunks of the palms visible on either side - the hillside and the lettering are NOT yet in shot. From 1.0s it begins ONE SLOW CONTINUOUS RISE, lifting steadily upward and drifting slightly back, never stopping, never pausing, never speeding up. As it climbs, the near palms slide down and past the edges of frame while the distant hillside stays almost still - a strong parallax between foreground and background. By 3.0s the camera has risen clear of the palm crowns and the full hillside with the BOOTCAMP lettering is revealed across the top of the frame. It keeps rising gently to the end. It never cuts, never pushes in, never racks focus and never tilts down again. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-1.0s Low on the road. @image2 ENTERS FRAME from the bottom edge, close and large, passing the camera with its exhaust note filling the shot, and starts away down the boulevard. 1.0-2.2s THE RISE BEGINS. The car pulls ahead down the centre of the road, getting smaller. The palm trunks on both sides sweep down out of frame as the camera climbs. 2.2-3.8s THE REVEAL. The camera clears the palm crowns and the brown hillside opens up across the background with the huge white BOOTCAMP letters standing on it, hazy in the late light. The car is well down the boulevard now, one small black shape among ordinary traffic, still driving toward it. 3.8-5.0s The rise continues gently. The car keeps going, small now, heading straight down the boulevard toward the hill and the lettering. Nothing else happens. THE PARALLAX IS THE POINT: the palms are close and move fast, the hillside is far and moves almost not at all. The difference between them is what gives the rise its depth. The car shrinks steadily as it travels; the lettering grows steadily as the camera clears the trees. PHYSICS AND SCALE: the car has real mass and moves at an ordinary city speed, neither racing nor crawling. It stays in its lane, does not weave, does not accelerate hard. The street is alive around it - other traffic moving normally in both directions, nothing dramatic. Real haze and depth between camera and hillside. NOBODY SPEAKS. No dialogue, ad-libs, shouts, laughs or offscreen voices. SFX - a low V8 exhaust note passing close to camera and then receding down the boulevard, ordinary city traffic, a distant horn, warm wind, the general hum of a wide street late in the day. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: the rise is ONE unbroken move that never stops, reverses, pauses or speeds up, and the camera never tilts back down, never pushes in, never racks focus and never cuts. The lettering on the hill is revealed only by the camera clearing the trees and never appears in the first second. The car never stops, never turns off, never crashes, never speeds, never does a burnout and never leaves the boulevard - it simply drives away down the road. Nobody gets out and nobody is seen clearly through the glass. The hillside lettering never changes, flickers or animates. No drone-style swooping, no orbit, no crane arc - the movement is a straight steady rise. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze. No lens flare timed to the reveal. No relighting.

No dialogue, voiceover, ad-libs, laughs or offscreen voices of any kind at any point. SFX - the general quiet of a roadside evening: warm wind across open ground, the faint electrical hum of neon, the muffled murmur of a room full of people heard through glass, cutlery and plates faint and far away, a single car passing on the distant road, crickets starting up. Everything soft, distant and unhurried. The sound fades down with the picture. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: NOBODY IS EVER VISIBLE - no person walks into frame, stands on the forecourt, opens a door, gets into a vehicle or is identifiable through the windows. Neither vehicle moves, starts, has headlights on or is approached by anyone. The camera NEVER rises, sinks, tilts, pans, turns, arcs, orbits, swoops or pushes back in - it retreats in a straight line at constant speed and never stops before the fade. No drone-style movement of any kind. The sign never changes, flickers or animates. No text, titles, credits or logos appear on screen. No slow motion, speed ramp or freeze. No gimbal, dolly wobble, crane arc or handheld shake. No relighting.

## FULL PROMPT

10S · ONE TAKE · 5 REFERENCES

40mm Kodak 500T. handheld cinematography. Low golden-hour sun raking across the street from the side, long shadows, warm skin tones, cool skylight in the shadows. Photorealistic live-action, raw documentary realism. No 3D render, no game engine, no plastic skin, no retouch. 24fps, 180-degree shutter, natural motion blur. SFX only no music. No subtitles. American accent. 10s. @image1 - THE LOCATION AND THE EXACT DIRECTION THE CAMERA IS FACING. Use it for everything: a quiet residential Los Angeles street at the end of the day, an old pale green wooden bungalow with a low porch, turned porch posts, a wooden railing, peeling paint, a bit of graffiti on the siding, worn concrete steps down to a patchy lawn, chain-link fences, telephone poles and wires, palms and a hazy downtown skyline far behind, an old green sedan parked at the kerb. @image2 - SITTING ON THE PORCH STEPS in the middle: broad white man, spiked bleached blond hair, BURGUNDY paisley bandana tied round his head, narrow rectangular BLACK sunglasses with opaque lenses, heavy jaw, thin scar under his right eye, BURGUNDY sleeveless quilted vest over a white tee, grey tattoo sleeve, BEIGE CARGO TROUSERS and white sneakers. He is elbows on knees, hunched, in a foul mood. @image3 - sitting a step above and to one side, leaning back against a porch post: Black man, long slouched black beanie, thin moustache and small chin beard, narrow dark sunglasses, BURGUNDY coach jacket over a long white tee, beige cargo trousers, a skateboard propped beside him. @image4 - standing at the bottom of the steps on the other side, one boot up on the lowest step: large heavyset Black man, full round afro, thick full black beard, BURGUNDY paisley bandana round his forehead, black denim trucker jacket over a long white tee. @image5 - THE RING, revealed at 4.2s: a massive gold signet ring densely pave-set with small stones across its whole face, catching the low sun in hundreds of tiny warm points. It sits in @image2's open palm. All cards give appearance ONLY. Do NOT reproduce sheet layout, multiple views, panels, captions or backdrop. STRICT: EXACTLY THREE PEOPLE, all in BURGUNDY and black, sitting and standing around the porch steps the way people hang out on a street at the end of the day. Nobody else is on the street. No crowd, no extras, no duplicates. CAMERA - ONE CONTINUOUS TAKE, NO CUTS, NO SECOND ANGLE. 40mm, chest height, a medium framing on the three of them at the porch steps. Shoulder-mounted handheld with soft weight, gentle float. At 4.2s it PUSHES IN slowly onto @image2's open palm until the ring nearly fills the frame. At 6.0s it PULLS BACK OUT and at the same time SINKS SLIGHTLY DOWN, settling into a low framing that holds all three of them standing and sitting around the ring, looking down at it. It stays there. It never pans away, never racks focus and never cuts. ACTION, TIMED: 0.0-0.8s The three of them are sitting around on the steps in the late sun, saying nothing. @image2 is hunched over with his elbows on his knees, radiating bad mood. 0.8-2.0s @image3, flat, genuinely just reporting it: "Bro, that girl folded you." 2.0-2.8s @image4 lets out one short low laugh. @image2 does not move. 2.8-4.2s @image2, without looking up, sour: "Laugh it up, clowns." Then his hand goes into the pocket of his vest. 4.2-6.0s HE OPENS HIS PALM. @image5 sits in it, throwing off tiny points of light. THE CAMERA PUSHES IN on it. @image2, quiet and pleased with himself: "Look what I took off the fat guy." 6.0-7.4s THE CAMERA PULLS BACK

AND DROPS LOW. @image3 leans in off the step; @image4 comes down and in from the other side. All three are now around the ring, heads angled down at it. 7.4-8.6s Nobody says anything. @image2's mouth pulls into a slow crooked grin. @image4 laughs again, lower and nastier this time. @image3 just stares at the ring with his mouth slightly open. 8.6-9.4s @image2 lifts his head and LOOKS STRAIGHT DOWN THE LENS - the only time in the film he acknowledges the camera at all. He is still grinning. 9.4-10.0s He reaches up and PUTS HIS PALM OVER THE LENS. His hand fills the frame and everything goes to BLACK. ACTING: this is three men on a street who have lost everything today and just found a reason to try again. @image2's eyes are hidden behind opaque lenses so the shift reads in the mouth and the shoulders: hunched and sour at the start, straightening as the ring comes out, grinning by the end. @image3's line is not a joke to him, he is just saying what happened. @image4's two laughs are different: the first is at his own boss, the second is at whoever they are going to use the ring on. Nobody explains where the ring came from. Natural skin with visible pores and faint shine in the low sun, no retouching. ONLY @image2 AND @image3 SPEAK, AND ONLY THE LINES ABOVE. @image4 never speaks; his laughter is laughter, not words. No ad-libs, mumbling, grunts, breaths-as-voice or extra jeering. Every beat lands on the tail of the one before. SFX - a quiet residential street at dusk: distant traffic, a dog somewhere, wind in palms, a car passing a block away, fabric shifting, two men laughing, the tiny metallic sound of a heavy ring being taken out of a pocket, then a soft muffled thump as a palm covers the lens and everything cuts to silence. No music. STRICT NEGATIVES: @image2 is fully dressed and wearing trousers throughout - no boxer shorts, no printed underwear and no bare legs at any point. Nobody explains where the ring came from and nobody says the name of the man it belonged to beyond the one line. @image2 never removes his sunglasses, never stands up fully, never puts the ring on his finger and never uses it. The ring never glows, sparks, activates, emits anything or transforms anything. Nobody else appears on the street. The camera never cuts, never pans away, never racks focus and never returns after the hand covers it - the picture ends in black. His hand covers the lens completely and the black is total.
